I don’t know if I’d say I don’t enjoy it at all… it just feels more muted.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed that most days, you feel down or sad most of the day, not just for a short while?\n\nPatient: Yes. Most days I wake up with that heavy, hollow sadness, and it tends to stay with me for a lot of the day. It’s not just a quick feeling that passes.\n\nDoctor: Do you feel this heavy sadness most of the day, nearly every day, or does it come and go more noticeably?\n\nPatient: It’s more like most of the day. I wake up with it, that heavy hollow feeling, and it kind of stays with me. Like I said, it’s like the studio lights are on but there’s no music in me.\n\nDoctor: Have you experienced a loss of interest or pleasure in dancing or other activities you used to enjoy, nearly every day?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure I’d say nearly every day. I have noticed it feels harder to really enjoy dancing the way I used to, but I can’t tell if it’s that constant or that clear-cut.\n\nDoctor: Would you say that you experience this loss of enjoyment in dancing and other activities you used to like on most days, nearly every day, rather than just occasionally?\n\nPatient: I’m not totally sure. It does happen, like dancing doesn’t feel as satisfying sometimes, but I don’t know if I can say it’s most days or nearly every day. It’s hard for me to pin down.\n\nDoctor: Would you say that on most days, nearly every day, you experience a loss of interest or pleasure in dancing and other activities you used to enjoy?\n\nPatient: I honestly can’t say it’s nearly every day. I do have times where dancing feels kind of flat, like I’m just going through the motions, but I haven’t really tracked it enough to say it’s most days.\n\nDoctor: Would you say that on most days, nearly every day, you experience a loss of interest or pleasure in dancing and other activities you used to enjoy?\n\nPatient: I still don’t think I can say “nearly every day.” I’ve noticed it comes up sometimes, where dancing or other things don’t feel as enjoyable, but I’m not sure it’s that frequent.\n\nDoctor: Would you say that on most days, nearly every day, you experience a loss of interest or pleasure in dancing and other activities you used to enjoy?\n\nPatient: I don’t think I can say that, no.
